Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in homologies theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 183 and the invariant I(A,B) = 373 mod 5.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the homologies decomposition. By minimality, the element a^183 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 373-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
